ParlaMint-PL_2017-11-23-sejm-52-2.u653	Thank you, Member. I would like to speak with the Undersecretary of State at the Ministry of Family, Labour and Social Policy of Mr Marcin Zielenecki. You're welcome, Minister.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-11-23-sejm-52-2.u654	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the pleasure and the honour to present the draft position of the Council of Ministers in relation to today's parliamentary bill, prepared by the Ministry of Family, Labour and Social Policy. Allow me to present the most important parts of this position, also given the broadness of the opinion. In 2017, two important social security projects were implemented from the point of view of the Polish society. The first is to restore the retirement age to 60 years for women and 65 years for men. The second is an increase in the lowest State guaranteed benefits. However, the restoration of the freedom of choice for each insured person to take an individual decision at the time of exercising his right to a pension and the termination of his professional activity entails certain consequences. The increase in the number of pensions paid by the Social Security Office at the end of December 2017, linked to the introduction of a change in the retirement age, is estimated at around 331 thousand, assuming that it will immediately retire around 82% persons entitled to this benefit. In the following years, the impact of the proposals will gradually decrease: 148 000. in 2018, 90 000 in 2019, 92 000 in 2020 and 54 000 In 2021, the financial impact of restoring the retirement age is estimated at PLN 2182 million in 2017, PLN 10 448 million in 2018 and PLN 11 798 million in 2019. According to the forecasts made by the Social Insurance Institution when preparing the government's position on the draft law restoring the retirement age of the Social Insurance Fund's expenditure to the end of 2012, and the increase in the negative balance of the FUS will occur mainly in the first two to three years after the amendment was introduced. Then there will be a stabilisation and even a gradual decline in the negative balance due to the decrease in the retirement age. In 2017, an informed decision was also taken to increase the lowest pension benefits paid in the amount guaranteed by the State. In order to improve the living conditions of the poorest seniors from 1 March 2017, the lowest pensions, pensions for total incapacity for work and survivor's pensions, paid both in the general system and in the agricultural system, were increased from PLN 882,56 to PLN 1000, i.e. by PLN 117.44, i.e. by 13.3%, the social pension of PLN 741,35 up to the amount of PLN 840, i.e. by 13,3%, and pensions for partial incapacity for work of PLN 676,75 up to the amount of PLN 750, i.e. 10.8%. The lowest pensions therefore increased by 13,31% and not, as would be the case with the valorisation mechanism, by 0,44%, i.e. 3.88 PLN. The cost of valorisation of benefits with a guaranteed increase of at least PLN 10 with an increase in the lowest benefits in 2017 will be over PLN 2 billion. In addition, from 1 March 2018, pursuant to the Act of 29 September 2017, with the compensation from March 2017 to the amount of the lowest guaranteed benefits will be raised 105 thousand. acquired pensions to which the right has been acquired ex officio. In the case of an ex officio pension granted in exchange for a pension for total incapacity for work, the monthly increase in benefits will be PLN 107.44 gross, the compensation for a period of 12 months will be PLN 1289.28 as regards pensions, while in the case of a replacement from a pension for a partial incapacity for work, the amount of PLN 63,25 and PLN 759 is included. From 1 March 2018, all benefits will be increased as part of the valorisation rate currently projected at 102.7%. This means that the lowest pension benefits will increase from PLN 1000 to PLN 1027, i.e. by PLN 27, the average pension will increase from PLN 2163.59 to PLN 2222.01, i.e. by PLN 58,42, the average disability pension will increase from PLN 1611 to almost PLN 1655, i.e. by over PLN 43.51, the average family pension will increase from PLN 1888.68 to PLN 1939.67, i.e. Approximately PLN 50.99. Total cost of valorisation of pension benefits and other benefits of the general, agricultural and uniform system at 102.7% is estimated at about PLN 5400 million. This is the result in 10 months from March to December. Any further idea resulting in an increase in expenditure and an increase in the burden on the state budget should be preceded by a detailed analysis taking into account the state finances and the potential of the state budget and the expectations of society as a whole. First of all, the annual payment of allowances requires an estimate of the expected cost not only in one year but in the long term of at least 10 years. The impact of the proposed solution on the situation of public finances and the state of the state budget, on the development of the economy and on pension farms should also be assessed. Additional support for the poorest social groups certainly deserves a positive assessment, but it is now too early to decide on a positive consideration of the proposal for permanent cash additions. The granting of fixed annual allowances to a wide group of recipients is too heavy a burden on the state budget. The proposed different dates for the payment of allowances for each eligible group or the unclear terms and conditions for granting and paying them raise doubts. The bill needs to be refined both logically and legislatively. It should be noted that the financial plans of the FUS and the pension fund for 2017 and 2018 do not take into account the payment of these benefits and do not provide for an increased grant from the State budget for this purpose. There is also a shortage of funds in the pension expenditure of uniformed services and in the Labour Fund plan. Nor does the State budget provide for increased expenditure on benefits for combatants and war and military invalids and those entitled to social pensions. Thanks to stable economic policy, the state can afford to introduce pro-social programmes and carry out activities that serve a large part of Polish families, eliminating poverty and creating a chance for a decent life. The proposed solution contained in the parliamentary project does not take into account the current social security fund and the budget of the State of increased expenditure on socially important solutions introduced in 2017. The restoration of the retirement age to 60 for women and 65 for men has become a fact and the cost of significantly increasing the lowest benefits paid in the State guaranteed amount increases the expenditure of the FUS and the state budget in the following years. The State budget policy must be implemented using financial constraints under national and Union law, including the need to apply the upper limit of State budget expenditure established on the basis of a stabilising expenditure rule, as well as respect for the reference values for the nominal deficit and government debt, and implementation of the so-called. the medium-term budgetary objective. In connection with the functioning of the legal system, the expenditure rule is not free to shape expenditure of the public finance sector. Currently, there is no financial possibility to introduce the proposed solution contained in the parliamentary bill. Conclusion. In view of the above, the Council of Ministers negatively assesses the parliamentary draft law on the fixed allowance for certain pensioners, pensioners and persons receiving pre-retirement benefits, pre-retirement benefits, bridge pensions and teacher compensation benefits. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-11-23-sejm-52-2.u699	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Poselski Club I am proud to present my position on the report of the committee on the Parliamentary draft law on the amendment of the Nature Conservation Act and the Act on the protection of monuments and the protection of monuments, prints No. 1977 and 2019. The government amendment of the Act on the Protection of Monuments and the Protection of Monuments implemented on 9 September 2017 has caused a lot of harmful confusion. It opened a gate for illegal logging of trees in these areas, because it did not include penalties either under the rules laid down in the Nature Conservation Act or on any other. It introduced provisions that removing a tree from a property entered in the register of monuments would not bear any fees that would have been necessary if the tree had grown on a property not entered in that register. The requirement for replacement plantings in historical areas has also disappeared. The proposed changes in the parliamentary project, print No 1977, are to correct this situation. The consent of the provincial monument conservator will continue to be required to remove trees or shrubs in historical areas. However, the developer proposes that the conservator should no longer issue an additional authorisation under conservation regulations for these areas. This will be done on the basis of the Nature Conservation Act, which regulates this issue in more detail than the Landmark Protection Act. The project therefore brings together issues which are subject to the competence of two separate entities. Importantly, there will be no controversial exemption from the obligation to have trees cut out for trees growing in the conservation area. Thus, the Nature Conservation Act is to be leading in terms of tree clippings in all areas, which positively changes the situation introduced by the 9 September amendment, when the historic areas were removed from under - already reduced by lex Szyszko - the protection provided by the Nature Conservation Act. The proposed amendment should not result in an increased cut-off or destruction of nature. For this reason, the Minister of the Environment's regulation on criteria for recognising living and inanimate nature objects as natural monuments, presented at the end of September this year, raises uncertainty. The tree circuits proposed by Minister Szyszko, which are to be a criterion for monumental protection, do not correspond to the circuits of most existing natural monuments. According to the Association's calculations, the city Tree, for example, in the city of Lądek-Zdrój on 87 monumental trees, only four meet the new limits. In addition, the Minister for the Environment reduces the fees for the cut-off in the Regulation up to 20 and 30 times. According to the proposed amendments to the Act on the Protection of Monuments and the Protection of Monuments, the permission of the Provincial Monument Preserver requires the conduct of conservation, restoration or construction works at the monument entered in the register, including works consisting of the removal of a tree or shrub from a property or part of it which is entered in the register by a park, garden or other form of greenery designed. Actions to remove trees or shrubs from the property area or parts thereof which are not included in the register by park, garden or other form of designed greenery shall be exempted from the licence of the conservator. The Modern Posel Club will vote in favour of the project.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-11-23-sejm-52-2.u700	Position of the Parliamentary Club of the Polish People's Party on the amendment of the Nature Conservation Act and the Act on the Protection of Monuments and the Protection of Monuments. ParlaMint-PL_2017-11-23-sejm-52-2.u701	Position of the Civic Platform Club on printing 1977. The Civic Platform Club accepts the proposed solutions and recommends the adoption of the proposed law.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-11-23-sejm-52-2.u702	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I present the position of the Free and Solidarnia Circle for the report of the Committee on Culture and Media and the Committee on the Environment, Natural Resources and Forestry on the draft law amending the Act on Nature Conservation and the Act on the Protection of Monuments and the Protection of Monuments. The bill is aimed at ordering the rules on the removal of trees and shrubs from the property listed in the register of monuments. The rules governing these issues have been amended several times and need to be coherent. Currently, there are two separate systems of administrative decisions empowering the owner of the property to cut the tree. The first is based on the provisions of the Nature Conservation Act, which regulates the removal of trees or shrubs from the property area or parts thereof not included in the register of monuments, and the complementary provisions contain the Act on the protection of monuments and the protection of monuments, which includes the rules for the removal of trees or shrubs from the property entered in the register of monuments. The intention of the designers is to combine both systems, so that the removal of trees or shrubs from the property area or part thereof entered in the register of monuments is subject to the provisions of the Nature Conservation Act, which, contrary to the Act on the Protection of Monuments, is a comprehensive regulation containing provisions on fees and administrative penalties for logging, making plantings and a catalogue of derogations specifying cases where the removal of the tree or shrub does not require the approval of the public administration. As a result of the changes made, the logging of trees or shrubs from the site of the property or part thereof entered in the register of monuments will be carried out on the basis of the permission of the voivodeship conservationist of monuments issued under the provisions of the Nature Conservation Act. Except for the removal of a tree or shrub from a property or part thereof which is entered in the register by a park, garden or other form of designed greenery, it will not require an additional permission from the voivodeship conservationist of monuments issued under the provisions of the Act on the Protection of Monuments. The proposal simplifies and harmonises the existing rules and, for this reason, deserves support. About Free l Solidarni will vote in favour of the bill.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-11-23-sejm-52-2.u714	Thank you. Mrs. Marshal! Ministers! High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club of the Polish People's Party, I have the honour to present a position towards the government draft law on amending the Law on special arrangements for implementing the budget law for the current year, i.e. 2017. The bill gives the possibility to finance some of the tasks of the state budget this year, from the resources that are savings in the special provisions, i.e. from part 83 of the budget. The government, through this project, envisages redirecting these savings, the 2017 special reserves to the following objectives. The highest amount, i.e. up to PLN 1 billion, will be directed to the National Health Fund to finance over-executions in the field of health care services related to hospitalization for internal diseases, orthopaedics, traumatology performed in an emergency mode, as well as oncology and oncology services of high-specialist, transplant, cardiological and cardiosurgical care provided in a life-saving manner. The value of these unfunded overperformances amounts to PLN 1334 million for benefits made before 2017 and for benefits from the current year for 9 months amounts to PLN 1708 million. So it is entirely justified in our view to direct this money to health care. The amount of 200 million will be allocated to the Reprivatisation Fund for investment tasks in the field of maritime affairs, the amount of 180 million PLN - to the Thermomodernisation and Renovation Fund, while the amount of 100 million will be allocated to the Agency for Restructuring and Modernization of Agriculture for financial assistance to agricultural producers who have suffered damage to crops, forests or buildings which serve to carry out their agricultural activities caused by storms occurring in August this year. Well, maybe it's a little late, but if the government has an idea to pay those victims, I don't think there's anything to pick on. The amount of up to PLN 79 million will be used to purchase real estate for the Central Anti-corruption Office. The bill also provides for the exemption of the Public Prosecutor's Office from the obligation to participate in the settlement process. According to the assumptions of this project, in the case of funding over-executions by the National Health Fund, the amount made available amounts to up to PLN 1 billion, and these overexecutions, as we have calculated, exceed PLN 3 billion, so that the settlement will be in the form of settlements with the provider. Therefore, the Attorney General is obliged to participate in the formulation of these arrangements. Such an expectation of participation in this trial of the Attorney General can indeed prolong and complicate the entire process of payment of these excesses. We therefore believe that it is absolutely justified to waive this requirement. It is unique, in a specific period and we do not see any major problems related to it. The Parliamentary Club of the Polish People's Party is in favour of directing the project to further work in the committee. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-11-23-sejm-52-2.u785	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the club of the Polish People's Party, I will present my position on the report of the Senate's resolution on the Act amending the Law on the Protection of Mental Health and some other laws. According to the amendment, the admission to the social assistance home of a person without its consent will require the decision of the guardian court to refer the person to such a home, with the consent of its statutory representative. The new rules are intended to bring Polish law into line with the judgments of the European Court of Human Rights and the Constitutional Tribunal. In addition, a periodic mental examination of a person in a social assistance home was introduced in order to justify his or her stay in such a facility. Studies would be carried out at least every 6 months. In order to ensure better legal protection for a person who is directed without his consent to a psychiatric hospital, an obligation to appoint a lawyer or counsel has been introduced. The Tribunal shall also be required to establish a lawyer or counsel of its own motion if he considers his participation to be necessary and the person concerned will not be able to make an appropriate application. In the course of its work, the Commission adopted an amendment on the issue of parental consent to place the child in a treatment facility without the need for judicial consent and legislative and editorial amendments. The Senate introduced one amendment to the bill. According to this amendment, the statutory representative's consent to the admission of a minor to a social assistance home, even if it remains under parental authority, will require authorisation from the custody court. The aim of the novel is to increase the protection of people with mental disorders directed without their consent to social assistance homes and those to whom direct coercion applies. The admission to the home of a person's social assistance without his consent, and with the consent of his or her statutory representative, will require a decision by a court of custody. The Polish People's Party Club will vote in accordance with the opinion of the committee responsible.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-11-23-sejm-52-2.u807	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On November 13th he visited Opole land, in particular in the district of Krapkowice the President of the Republic of Andrzej Duda. It was a visit from a series of visitations of counties in which he was not during the election campaign, and in which he promised that he would try to visit all the counties of our country. However, my speech concerns not so much a visit to Krapkowice, but the fact that the President extended the program by a very personal visit to the Silesian Stone, the birthplace of St. Jack Odrowie. It is a small town located approx. 30 km from Opole. However, he prides himself on the beautifully rebuilt Bishop's curia, namely the great effort of Fr. Archbishop Alfonso Nosol, then chief of Opole, the palace of Odrowążów. The palace survived World War II in an almost intact state. It was only partially plundered by the Russian troops in 1945. After the war, the children's home was arranged there, but in the late 1950s the Soviets returned to the nearby airport and the palace was dedicated to military housing. In 1970, leaving the army, after the previous destruction, the palace burned down, which burned to the ground. In 1990 the ruins of the palace were handed over to the Opole diocese. In 1994 the Bishop of Opole established the Sanctuary of St. Jack - Centre for Culture and Science of the Theological Faculty of the University of Opole. Since then, the rank of the Silesian Stone has increased, becoming a place more and more visited by numerous people, and numerous conferences and symposiums are held in the palace. However, the main magnet drawing to the Silesian Stone is the Sanctuary of St. Jack Odrowie. St. Jacek was born around 1183. This family is still born of Bl. Czesław and Bl. Bronisława. He studied at the Cracow cathedral school, which he graduated from in 1219. In 1220 he arrived in Rome, where at the request of St. Dominica joined the newly formed preaching order, or Dominicans. In the spring of 1221 he and his companions went to Poland, going through Bologna, Padua, Treviso, Austria, Czech Republic and Moravia. On his way he founded several Dominican Orders, among others. in Wroclaw. On 1 November 1221 he arrived in Krakow. Jacek Odrowąż became the superior of Polish Dominicans. In 1225 he arrived in Mazovia. In May 1228 he attended a meeting of the Dominican General Capital in Paris. In the fall of 1228 he went with his comrades to Russia towards Kiev. In 1232 the Provincial Chapter decided that Jacek Odrowąż should go to Prussia, convert the Prusai, pastoralize the knights who came to the crusade. After 1242, he returned to Cracow, where he spent the last years of his life, still evangelizing and preaching. He died on 15 August 1257. Its tomb is located in the Dominican Basilica of the Holy Trinity in Cracow. He was blessed in 1527. It was canonized on 17 April 1594 in the Basilica of St. Peter in Rome by Pope Clement VIII. St. Jacek is one of the greatest saints of the Polish Church. One could say today that he was a true European, as I mentioned earlier, he has traveled almost all over Europe. St. Jacek Odrowąż is the only saint in Poland, the only saint in this part of Europe, who was immortalized in the Berlin colonnade around St. Peter in Rome. That is why I thank the President of the Republic of Poland in particular from this place, that he has found time to make it so important for Poland to visit the place where this great saint was born, especially since the visit to the Silesian Stone was not planned before. For he haunted the grave of St. Jack in Cracow. So the visitation of his birth was a kind of complement. And finally, I'm going to quote Fr. Prime Minister Dr. Albert Glezer, curator of the sanctuary, who wished for the President, quote: The message of the President for Poland and for the Church, and above all for the other man, let it be supported by the message of this great man. End of quote. Thank you for your attention. [[Applause]]
